Much like Emrakul's warping influence, Pro Tour *Eldritch Moon* has come and gone, leaving a very different Standard in its wake. Even if you don't have the cards in your collection, you can test drive some of the best and most interesting decks from the latest Pro Tour in Pro Tour Gauntlet events on *Magic Online*! Pro Tour Gauntlet events for Pro Tour: *Eldritch Moon* run from the August 10 downtime until August 17. Event details and available decklists can be found below!

*   [Pro Tour Gauntlet Event Details](#PTGauntletEMNEvents)
*   [Pro Tour Gauntlet Decklists](#Decks)

* * *

**Pro Tour Gauntlet**

**Start Times:** Wednesday, August 10, after downtime until Wednesday, August 17, downtime.  
**Location:** Play Lobby -> Constructed Tournaments -> Queues  
**Entry Option:**

*   6 Event Tickets
*   60 Play Points

**Product:** One randomly-selected phantom deck from the Pro Tour Gauntlet decklists. Phantom product will not be added to a player's collection at the conclusion of the event.  
**Size:** 8 players  
**Play Style:** Swiss  
**Format:** Standard  
**Duration:** 3 minutes deck review. Three rounds, each round up to 50 minutes.  
**Prizes:**

<table><thead><tr><th scope="col">Place</th>&#10;			<th scope="col">Prizes</th>&#10;			<th scope="col">QPs</th>&#10;		</tr></thead><colgroup><col span="3"></colgroup><tbody><tr><td>3 Wins</td>&#10;			<td>90 Play Points</td>&#10;			<td>1</td>&#10;		</tr><tr><td>2 Wins</td>&#10;			<td>60 Play Points</td>&#10;			<td>0</td>&#10;		</tr><tr><td>1 Win</td>&#10;			<td>20 Play Points</td>&#10;			<td>0</td>&#10;		</tr></tbody></table>

**NOTE:** Wizards of the Coast employees and other persons affiliated with it are eligible to play in this event. For more information, please see [this article](/en/articles/feature/2015/01-30-wizards-announces-wizards-play-policy) and [this FAQ](https://wizards.custhelp.com/app/answers/detail/a_id/2312) about this policy.
